hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if       log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n \u89e3\u6790\n    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n\n    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n    return {\n self.ub = ub\n    32\t\n    33\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None):\n    34\t        dim, lb, ub = self.dim, self.lb, self.ub\n    35\t        ps = self.pop_size\n    36\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed if seed is not None else _config_seed)\n    37\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (ps, dim))\n    38\t        evals = 0; bCR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n    58\t\n    59\t            prog = min(1.0, evals / max(1, self.max_evals))\n    60\t            target = max(p_min, int(round(p_init + (p_min - p_init)*prog)))\n    61\t            if target < p_cur:\n    62\t                ki = np.argsort(fit)[:target]\n    63\t                pop = pop[ki].copy(); fit = fit[ki]; p_cur = target\n    64\t\n    65\t            p = max(0.05, 0.2*(1.0-prog)); npb = max(2, int(p_cur*p))\n    66\t            sidx = np.argsort(fit)[:npb]\n    67\t\n    68\t            mi = rng.randint(0, msize, size=p_cur)\n    69\t            F_i = np.clip(mF[mi] + 0.1*rng.standard_cauchy(p_cur), 0.1, 1.0)\n    70\t            CR_i = np.clip(mCR[mi] + 0.1*rng.randn(p_cur), 0.0, 1.0)\n    71\t            if self.controller is not None:\n    72\t                F_i = np.clip(0.7*F_i + 0.3*bF, 0.05, 1.0)\n    73\t                CR_i = np.clip(0.7*CR_i + 0.3*bCR, 0.0, 1.0)\n    74\t\n    75\t            pbi = sidx[rng.randint(0, npb, size=p_cur)]\n    76\t            r1 = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=p_cur)\n    77\t            same = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\n    78\t            while np.any(same):\n    79\t                r1[same] = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=int(np.sum(same))); bad = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\n    86\t            pa = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if na > 0 else pop\n    87\t            xr2 = pa[np.minimum(r2, len(pa)-1)]\n    88\t\n    89\t            mut = pop + F_i[:,None]*(pop[pbi]-pop) + F_i[:,None]*(pop[r1]-xr2)\n    90\t            for _ in range(5):\n    91\t                lo = mut < lb; evals += 1\n   105\t                if tf < fit[i]:\n   106\t                    SF.append(float(F_i[i])); SC.append(float(CR_i[i])); SD.append(float(fit[i]-tf))\n   107\t                    if len(archive) < arcap: archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1,-1)])\n   108\t                    else: archive[rng.randint(0, arcap)] = pop[i]\n   109\t                    pop[i] = trial[i]; mCR[midx] = max(0.0, min(1.0, mc))\n   119\t                midx = (midx+1) % msize\n   120\t\n   121\t            if gbest_f < prev_f - 1e-12: stag = 0\n   122\t            else:\n   123\t                stag += 1\n   124\t                if stag >= 5:\n   125\t                    nr = max(1, int(p_cur*0.15)); wi = np.argsort(fit)[-nr:]\n   126\t                    sig = (ub-lb)*0.02*(1.0+prog)\n   127\t                    for idx in wi:\n   128\t                        if evals >= self.max_evals: break\n   129\t                        pop[idx] = np.clip(gbest + sig*rng.standard_normal(dim), lb, ub)\n   130\t                        fit[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx]); evals += 1\n   131\t                    stag = 0\n   132\t\n   133\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   134\t                imp = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_f else 0.0\n   135\t                nobs = {'iteration': it, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   136\t                        'initial_gbest_f': init_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   137\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   138\t                    self.controller.store_reward(imp, nobs, evals >= self.max_evals)\n   139\t            prev_f = gbest_f\n   140\t\n   141\t        self.stats = {'gbest': gbest.tolist(), 'gbest_f': float(gbest_f), 'evals': int(evals),\n   142\t                      'iterations': int(it), 'initial_gbest_f': float(init_f)}\n   143\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\n   144\t\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Default pop size 40 to match evaluator, or use swarm_size\n    47\t        self.pop_size = int(swarm_size) if swarm_size is not None else 40\n    48\t        # F and CR defaults\n    49\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.7))\n    50\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    51\t\n    52\t        # Bounds\n    53\t        lb = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t        ub = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    55\t        if lb.ndim == 0:\n    56\t            lb = np.full(self.dim, float(lb))\n    57\t        if ub.ndim == 0:\n    58\t            ub = np.full(self.dim, float(ub))\n    59\t        self.lb = lb\n    60\t        self.ub = ub\n    61\t\n    62\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None):\n    63\t        \"\"\"Run JADE until max_evals is reached.\n    64\t\n    65\t        Returns:\n    66\t            (gbest_list, stats_dict)\n    67\t        \"\"\"\n    68\t        dim = self.dim\n    69\t        lb = self.lb\n    70\t        ub = self.ub\n    71\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    72\t\n    73\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    74\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    75\t\n    76\t        # ---- Initialization ----\n    77\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\n    78\t        if x0 is not None:\n    79\t            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\n    80\t            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\n    81\t                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\n    82\t\n    83\t        evals = 0\n    84\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n    85\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n    86\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n    87\t                break\n    88\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n    89\t            evals += 1\n    90\t\n    91\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n    92\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n    93\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n    94\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n    95\t        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n    96\t\n    97\t        # ---- JADE memory and archive ----\n    98\t        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\n    99\t        archive_capacity = max(pop_size * 2, 20)\n   100\t        mu_F = float(self.F)\n   101\t        mu_CR = float(self.CR)\n   102\t        c = 0.1\n   103\t\n   104\t        iteration = 0\n   105\t        while evals < self.max_evals:\n   106\t            iteration += 1\n   107\t\n   108\t            # Controller-provided F/CR are blended into the adaptation memory\n   109\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   110\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   111\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   112\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   113\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   114\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   115\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   116\t            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   117\t            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   118\t            base_F = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_F + 0.5 * mu_F))\n   119\t            base_CR = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_CR + 0.5 * mu_CR))\n   120\t\n   121\t            # p-best selection pool (shrinks as budget is consumed)\n   122\t            progress = evals / max(1, self.max_evals)\n   123\t            p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n   124\t            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * p))\n   125\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   126\t\n   127\t            # F_i drawn from Cauchy around mu_F, truncated\n   137\t                F_i = base_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\n   138\t                F_i = float(np.clip(F_i, 0.1, 1.0))\n   139\t                # CR_i drawn from Normal around mu_CR, truncated\n   140\t                CR_i = base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn()\n   141\t                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\n   142\t\n   143\t                # Current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   144\t                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)]\n   145\t                r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   146\t                while r1 == i:\n   147\t                    r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   148\t                if n_arch > 1:\n   149\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, n_arch)\n   150\t                    x_r2 = archive_pop[r2]\n   151\t                else:\n   152\t                    r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   153\t                    while r2 == i or r2 == r1:\n   154\t                        r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n   155\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   156\t\n   157\t                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n   158\t\n   159\t                # Boundary repair: reflect\n   160\t                for _ in range(5):\n   161\t                    below = mutant < lb\n   162\t                    above = mutant > ub\n   163\t                    if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\n   164\t                        break\n   165\t                    mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\n   166\t                    mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\n   167\t                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n   168\t\n   169\t                # Binomial crossover\n   170\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\n   171\t                mask = rng.random(dim) < CR_i\n   172\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   173\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   174\t\n   175\t                # Evaluation and selection\n   176\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   177\t                    break\n   178\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n   179\t                evals += 1\n   180\t\n   181\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   182\t                    # Save the replaced individual to the external archive\n   183\t                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n   184\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n   185\t                    else:\n   186\t                        ridx = rng.randint(0, archive_capacity)\n   187\t                        archive[ridx] = pop[i]\n   188\t                    new_pop[i] = trial\n   189\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   190\t                    S_F.append(F_i)\n   191\t                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n   192\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   193\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   194\t                        gbest = trial.copy()\n   195\t\n   196\t            pop = new_pop\n   197\t\n   198\t            # Update success-history memory (Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic for CR)\n   199\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   200\t                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F)\n   201\t                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR)\n   202\t                denom = np.sum(S_F_arr)\n   203\t                if denom > 0:\n   204\t                    mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F + c * np.sum(S_F_arr ** 2) / denom\n   205\t                mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * mu_CR + c * np.mean(S_CR_arr)\n   206\t                mu_F = float(np.clip(mu_F, 0.1, 1.0))\n   207\t                mu_CR = float(np.clip(mu_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n   208\t\n   209\t            # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n        \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        dim = self.dim\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n\\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # ---- Initialization (including optional seeded x0) ----\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\\n        if x0 is not None:\\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\\n            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\\n                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\\n\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n        # ---- L-SHADE memory, archive and population reduction state ----\\n        memory_size = 10\\n        mem_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.7)\\n        mem_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        mem_idx = 0\\n        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\\n        archive_cap = max(pop_size * 2, 60)\\n        p_init = pop_size\\n        p_cur = pop_size\\n        p_min = max(4, int(0.15 * pop_size))\\n        stag = 0\\n        iteration = 0\\n\\n        while evals < self.max_evals:\\n            iteration += 1\\n\\n            # ---- Controller-provided F/CR are blended into adaptive memories ----\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n\\n            # ---- Linear population size reduction ----\\n            progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1, self.max_evals))\\n            target = max(p_min, int(round(p_init + (p_min - p_init) * progress)))\\n            if target < p_cur:\\n                keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target]\\n                pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\\n                fitness = fitness[keep_idx]\\n                p_cur = target\\n\\n            # ---- p-best selection pool (shrinks with budget) ----\\n            p_pool = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(p_cur * p_pool))\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:n_pbest]\\n\\n            # ---- Generate per-individual F and CR from memories ----\\n            mem_choices = rng.randint(0, memory_size, size=p_cur)\\n            F_ind = np.clip(mem_F[mem_choices] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(p_cur), 0.1, 1.0)\\n            CR_ind = np.clip(mem_CR[mem_choices] + 0.1 * rng.randn(p_cur), 0.0, 1.0)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                F_ind = np.clip(0.7 * F_ind + 0.3 * base_F, 0.05, 1.0)\\n                CR_ind = np.clip(0.7 * CR_ind + 0.3 * base_CR, 0.0, 1.0)\\n\\n            # ---- current-to-pbest/1 with archive ----\\n            pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest, size=p_cur)]\\n            r1 = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=p_cur)\\n            same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\\n            while np.any(same_r1):\\n                r1[same_r1] = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=int(np.sum(same_r1)))\\n                same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\\n\\n            archive_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\\n            n_archive_total = len(archive_pop)\\n            r2 = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=p_cur)\\n            if n_archive_total > 0:\\n                bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\\n                while np.any(bad_r2):\\n                    r2[bad_r2] = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=int(np.sum(bad_r2)))\\n                    bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\\n            x_r2 = archive_pop[np.minimum(r2, len(archive_pop) - 1)]\\n\\n            mutant = pop + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop) + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\\n\\n            # ---- Boundary repair by reflection (up to 5 times), then clip ----\\n            for _ in range(5):\\n                below = mutant < lb\\n                above = mutant > ub\\n                if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\\n                    break\\n                mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\\n                mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\\n            mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\\n\\n            # ---- Binomial crossover (each individual gets at least one component) ----\\n            j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim, size=p_cur)\\n            crossover_mask = rng.random((p_cur, dim)) < CR_ind[:, None]\\n            crossover_mask[np.arange(p_cur), j_rand] = True\\n            trial = np.where(crossover_mask, mutant, pop)\\n\\n            # ---- Evaluate trials, greedy selection, collect successes and archive ----\\n            S_F, S_CR, S_diff = [], [], []\\n            for i in range(p_cur):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    S_F.append(float(F_ind[i]))\\n                    S_CR.append(float(CR_ind[i]))\\n                    S_diff.append(float(fitness[i] - trial_f))\\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, archive_cap)] = pop[i]\\n                    pop[i] = trial[i]\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trial[i].copy()\\n\\n            # ---- Update success-history memories ----\\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\\n                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F)\\n                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR)\\n                S_diff_arr = np.array(S_diff)\\n                total_diff = float(np.sum(S_diff_arr))\\n                if total_diff > 1e-30:\\n                    weights = S_diff_arr / total_diff\\n                else:\\n                    weights = np.ones_like(S_diff_arr) / len(S_diff_arr)\\n                weighted_F_denom = float(np.sum(weights * S_F_arr))\\n                if weighted_F_denom > 1e-30:\\n                    new_mem_F = float(np.sum(weights * S_F_arr ** 2) / weighted_F_denom)\\n                else:\\n                    new_mem_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\\n                new_mem_CR = float(np.sum(weights * S_CR_arr))\\n                mem_F[mem_idx] = max(0.1, min(1.0, new_mem_F))\\n                mem_CR[mem_idx] = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_mem_CR))\\n                mem_idx = (mem_idx + 1) % memory_size\\n\\n            # ---- Stagnation handling: reinitialize worst individuals near current best ----\\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\\n                stag = 0\\n            else:\\n                stag += 1\\n                if stag >= 5:\\n                    n_refresh = max(1, int(p_cur * 0.15))\\n                    worst_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[-n_refresh:]\\n                    sigma = (ub - lb) * 0.02 * (1.0 + progress)\\n                    for idx in worst_idx:\\n                        if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                            break\\n                        pop[idx] = np.clip(gbest + sigma * rng.standard_normal(dim), lb, ub)\\n                        fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\\n                        evals += 1\\n                    stag = 0\\n\\n            # ---- Controller reward / transition storage ----\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, evals >= self.max_evals)\\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",
    "text": "<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n    \"tool_input\": {\n        \"command\": \"str_replace\",\n        \"path\": \"/hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\",\n        \"old_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n        \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        dim = self.dim\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n\\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # ---- Initialization (including optional seeded x0) ----\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\\n        if x0 is not None:\\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\\n            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\\n                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\\n\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n        # ---- L-SHADE memory, archive and population reduction state ----\\n        memory_size = 10\\n        mem_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.7)\\n        mem_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        mem_idx = 0\\n        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\\n        archive_cap = max(pop_size * 2, 60)\\n        p_init = pop_size\\n        p_cur = pop_size\\n        p_min = max(4, int(0.15 * pop_size))\\n        stag = 0\\n        iteration = 0\\n\\n        while evals < self.max_evals:\\n            iteration += 1\\n\\n            # ---- Controller-provided F/CR are blended into adaptive memories ----\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n\\n            # ---- Linear population size reduction ----\\n            progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1, self.max_evals))\\n            target = max(p_min, int(round(p_init + (p_min - p_init) * progress)))\\n            if target < p_cur:\\n                keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target]\\n                pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\\n                fitness = fitness[keep_idx]\\n                p_cur = target\\n\\n            # ---- p-best selection pool (shrinks with budget) ----\\n            p_pool = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(p_cur * p_pool))\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:n_pbest]\\n\\n            # ---- Generate per-individual F and CR from memories ----\\n            mem_choices = rng.randint(0, memory_size, size=p_cur)\\n            F_ind = np.clip(mem_F[mem_choices] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(p_cur), 0.1, 1.0)\\n            CR_ind = np.clip(mem_CR[mem_choices] + 0.1 * rng.randn(p_cur), 0.0, 1.0)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                F_ind = np.clip(0.7 * F_ind + 0.3 * base_F, 0.05, 1.0)\\n                CR_ind = np.clip(0.7 * CR_ind + 0.3 * base_CR, 0.0, 1.0)\\n\\n            # ---- current-to-pbest/1 with archive ----\\n            pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest, size=p_cur)]\\n            r1 = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=p_cur)\\n            same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\\n            while np.any(same_r1):\\n                r1[same_r1] = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=int(np.sum(same_r1)))\\n                same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\\n\\n            archive_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\\n            n_archive_total = len(archive_pop)\\n            r2 = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=p_cur)\\n            if n_archive_total > 0:\\n                bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\\n                while np.any(bad_r2):\\n                    r2[bad_r2] = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=int(np.sum(bad_r2)))\\n                    bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\\n            x_r2 = archive_pop[np.minimum(r2, len(archive_pop) - 1)]\\n\\n            mutant = pop + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop) + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\\n\\n            # ---- Boundary repair by reflection (up to 5 times), then clip ----\\n            for _ in range(5):\\n                below = mutant < lb\\n                above = mutant > ub\\n                if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\\n                    break\\n                mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\\n                mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\\n            mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\\n\\n            # ---- Binomial crossover (each individual gets at least one component) ----\\n            j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim, size=p_cur)\\n            crossover_mask = rng.random((p_cur, dim)) < CR_ind[:, None]\\n            crossover_mask[np.arange(p_cur), j_rand] = True\\n            trial = np.where(crossover_mask, mutant, pop)\\n\\n            # ---- Evaluate trials, greedy selection, collect successes and archive ----\\n            S_F, S_CR, S_diff = [], [], []\\n            for i in range(p_cur):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    S_F.append(float(F_ind[i]))\\n                    S_CR.append(float(CR_ind[i]))\\n                    S_diff.append(float(fitness[i] - trial_f))\\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, archive_cap)] = pop[i]\\n                    pop[i] = trial[i]\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trial[i].copy()\\n\\n            # ---- Update success-history memories ----\\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\\n                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F)\\n                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR)\\n                S_diff_arr = np.array(S_diff)\\n                total_diff = float(np.sum(S_diff_arr))\\n                if total_diff > 1e-30:\\n                    weights = S_diff_arr / total_diff\\n                else:\\n                    weights = np.ones_like(S_diff_arr) / len(S_diff_arr)\\n                weighted_F_denom = float(np.sum(weights * S_F_arr))\\n                if weighted_F_denom > 1e-30:\\n                    new_mem_F = float(np.sum(weights * S_F_arr ** 2) / weighted_F_denom)\\n                else:\\n                    new_mem_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\\n                new_mem_CR = float(np.sum(weights * S_CR_arr))\\n                mem_F[mem_idx] = max(0.1, min(1.0, new_mem_F))\\n                mem_CR[mem_idx] = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_mem_CR))\\n                mem_idx = (mem_idx + 1) % memory_size\\n\\n            # ---- p-best selection pool (shrinks with budget) ----\n   152\t            p_pool = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n   153\t            n_pbest = max(2, int(p_cur * p_pool))\n   154\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:n_pbest]\n   155\t\n   156\t            # ---- current-to-pbest/1 with archive ----\n   165\t            pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest, size=p_cur)]\n   166\t            r1 = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=p_cur)\n   167\t            same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\n   168\t            while np.any(same_r1):\n   169\t                r1[same_r1] = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=int(np.sum(same_r1)))\n   170\t                same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\n   171\t\n   172\t            archive_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\n   173\t            n_archive_total = len(archive_pop)\n   174\t            r2 = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=p_cur)\n   175\t            if n_archive_total > 0:\n   176\t                bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\n   177\t                while np.any(bad_r2):\n   178\t                    r2[bad_r2] = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=int(np.sum(bad_r2)))\n   179\t                    bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\n   180\t            x_r2 = archive_pop[np.minimum(r2, len(archive_pop) - 1)]\n   181\t\n   182\t            mutant = pop + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop) + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n   183\t\n   184\t            # ---- Boundary repair by reflection (up to 5 times), then clip ----\n   185\t            for _ in range(5):\n   186\t                below = mutant < lb\n   187\t                above = mutant > ub\n   188\t                if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\n   189\t                    break\n   190\t                mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\n   191\t                mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\n   192\t            mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n   193\t\n   194\t            # ---- Binomial crossover (each individual gets at least one component) ----\n   195\t            j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim, size=p_cur)\n   196\t            crossover_mask = rng.random((p_cur, dim)) < CR_ind[:, None]\n   197\t            crossover_mask[np.arange(p_cur), j_rand] = True\n   198\t            trial = np.where(crossover_mask, mutant, pop)\n   199\t\n   200\t            # ---- Evaluate trials, greedy selection, collect successes and archive ----\n   201\t            S_F, S_CR, S_diff = [], [], []\n   202\t            for i in range(p_cur):\n   203\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   204\t                    break\n   205\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial[i])\n   206\t                evals += 1\n   207\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   208\t                    S_F.append(float(F_ind[i]))\n   209\t                    S_CR.append(float(CR_ind[i]))\n   210\t                    S_diff.append(float(fitness[i] - trial_f))\n   211\t                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n   212\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n   213\t                    else:\n   214\t                        archive[rng.randint(0, archive_cap)] = pop[i]\n   215\t                    pop[i] = trial[i]\n   216\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   217\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   218\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   219\t                        gbest = trial[i].copy()\n   220\t\n   221\t            # ---- Update success-history memories ----\n   222\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   223\t                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F)\n   224\t                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR)\n   225\t                S_diff_arr = np.array(S_diff)\n   226\t                total_diff = float(np.sum(S_diff_arr))\n   227\t                if total_diff > 1e-30:\n   228\t                    weights = S_diff_arr / total_diff\n   229\t                else:\n   230\t                    weights = np.ones_like(S_diff_arr) / len(S_diff_arr)\n   231\t                weighted_F_denom = float(np.sum(weights * S_F_arr))\n   232\t                if weighted_F_denom > 1e-30:\n   233\t                    new_mem_F = float(np.sum(weights * S_F_arr ** 2) / weighted_F_denom)\n   234\t                else:\n   235\t                    new_mem_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\n   236\t                new_mem_CR = float(np.sum(weights * S_CR_arr))\n   237\t                mem_F[mem_idx] = max(0.1, min(1.0, new_mem_F))\n   238\t                mem_CR[mem_idx] = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_mem_CR))\n   239\t                mem_idx = (mem_idx + 1) % memory_size\n   240\t\n   241\t            # ---- Stagnation handling: reinitialize worst individuals near current best ----\n   242\t            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n   243\t                stag = 0\n   244\t            else:\n   245\t                stag += 1\n   246\t                if stag >= 5:\n   247\t                    n_refresh = max(1, int(p_cur * 0.15))\n   248\t                    worst_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[-n_refresh:]\n   249\t                    sigma = (ub - lb) * 0.02 * (1.0 + progress)\n   250\t                    for idx in worst_idx:\n   251\t                        if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   252\t                            break\n   253\t                        pop[idx] = np.clip(gbest + sigma * rng.standard_normal(dim), lb, ub)\n   254\t                        fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\n   255\t                        evals += 1\n   256\t                    stag = 0\n   257\t\n   258\t            # ---- Controller reward / transition storage ----\n   259\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   260\t                improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   261\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   262\t                            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   263\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   264\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, evals >= self.max_evals)\n   265\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   266\t\n   267\t        self.stats = {\n   268\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   269\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   270\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   271\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   272\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   273\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   274\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   275\t        }\n   276\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\nReview the changes and make sure they are as expected.     "text": "Here are the available tools:\n```\n{'name': 'bash', 'description': 'Run commands in a bash shell\\n* When invoking this tool, the contents of the \"command\" parameter does NOT need to be XML-escaped.\\n* You don\\'t have access to the internet via this tool.\\n* You do have access to a mirror of common linux and python packages via apt and pip.\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user.\\n* To inspect a particular line range of a file, e.g. lines 10-25, try \\'sed -n 10,25p /path/to/the/file\\'.\\n* Please avoid commands that may produce a very large amount of output.\\n* Please run long lived commands in the background, e.g. \\'sleep 10 &\\' or start a server in the background.', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'description': 'The bash command to run.'}}, 'required': ['command']}}\n\n{'name': 'editor', 'description': 'Custom editing tool for viewing, creating and editing files\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user\\n* If `path` is a file, `view` displays the result of applying `cat -n`. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'str_replace', 'path': '/hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py', 'old_str': \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\", 'new_str': \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        dim = self.dim\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n\\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # ---- Initialization (including optional seeded x0) ----\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\\n        if x0 is not None:\\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\\n            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\\n                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\\n\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n        # ---- L-SHADE memory, archive and population reduction state ----\\n        memory_size = 10\\n        mem_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.7)\\n        mem_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        mem_idx = 0\\n        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\\n        archive_cap = max(pop_size * 2, 60)\\n        p_init = pop_size\\n        p_cur = pop_size\\n        p_min = max(4, int(0.15 * pop_size))\\n        stag = 0\\n        iteration = 0\\n\\n        while evals < self.max_evals:\\n            iteration += 1\\n\\n            # ---- Controller-provided F/CR are blended into adaptive memories ----\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n\\n            # ---- Linear population size reduction ----\\n            progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1, self.max_evals))\\n            target = max(p_min, int(round(p_init + (p_min - p_init) * progress)))\\n            if target < p_cur:\\n                keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target]\\n                pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\\n                fitness = fitness[keep_idx]\\n                p_cur = target\\n\\n            # ---- p-best selection pool (shrinks with budget) ----\\n            p_pool = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(p_cur * p_pool))\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:n_pbest]\\n\\n            # ---- Generate per-individual F and CR from memories ----\\n            mem_choices = rng.randint(0, memory_size, size=p_cur)\\n            F_ind = np.clip(mem_F[mem_choices] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(p_cur), 0.1, 1.0)\\n            CR_ind = np.clip(mem_CR[mem_choices] + 0.1 * rng.randn(p_cur), 0.0, 1.0)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                F_ind = np.clip(0.7 * F_ind + 0.3 * base_F, 0.05, 1.0)\\n                CR_ind = np.clip(0.7 * CR_ind + 0.3 * base_CR, 0.0, 1.0)\\n\\n            # ---- current-to-pbest/1 with archive ----\\n            pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest, size=p_cur)]\\n            r1 = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=p_cur)\\n            same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\\n            while np.any(same_r1):\\n                r1[same_r1] = rng.randint(0, p_cur, size=int(np.sum(same_r1)))\\n                same_r1 = (r1 == np.arange(p_cur))\\n\\n            archive_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\\n            n_archive_total = len(archive_pop)\\n            r2 = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=p_cur)\\n            if n_archive_total > 0:\\n                bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\\n                while np.any(bad_r2):\\n                    r2[bad_r2] = rng.randint(0, max(1, n_archive_total), size=int(np.sum(bad_r2)))\\n                    bad_r2 = (r2 < p_cur) & ((r2 == np.arange(p_cur)) | (r2 == r1))\\n            x_r2 = archive_pop[np.minimum(r2, len(archive_pop) - 1)]\\n\\n            mutant = pop + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop) + F_ind[:, None] * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\\n\\n            # ---- Boundary repair by reflection (up to 5 times), then clip ----\\n            for _ in range(5):\\n                below = mutant < lb\\n                above = mutant > ub\\n                if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\\n                    break\\n                mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\\n                mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\\n            mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\\n\\n            # ---- Binomial crossover (each individual gets at least one component) ----\\n            j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim, size=p_cur)\\n            crossover_mask = rng.random((p_cur, dim)) < CR_ind[:, None]\\n            crossover_mask[np.arange(p_cur), j_rand] = True\\n            trial = np.where(crossover_mask, mutant, pop)\\n\\n            #   # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n    63\t        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    64\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    65\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    69\t\n    70\t\n    71\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    72\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    73\t\n    74\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    75\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    76\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    77\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    78\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    79\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    80\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    81\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    82\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    83\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    84\t    \"\"\"\n    85\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    86\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    87\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    88\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    89\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    90\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    91\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    92\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    93\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    94\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    95\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    96\t\n    97\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    98\t        self.it = 0\n    99\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   100\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   101\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   102\t\n   103\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   104\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   105\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   106\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   107\t\n   108\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   109\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   110\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   111\t\n   112\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   113\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   114\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   115\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   116\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   117\t            else:\n   118\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   119\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   120\t\n   121\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   122\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   123\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   124\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   125\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   126\t\n   127\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   128\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   129\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   130\t        else:\n   131\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   132\t\n   133\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   134\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   135\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   136\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   137\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   138\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   139\t\n   140\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   141\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   142\t        current_gbest_cv = {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    69\t\n    70\t\n    71\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    72\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    73\t\n    74\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    75\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    76\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    77\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    78\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    79\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    80\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    81\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    82\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    83\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    84\t    \"\"\"\n    85\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    86\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    87\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    88\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    89\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    90\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    91\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    92\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    93\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    94\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    95\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    96\t\n    97\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    98\t        self.it = 0\n    99\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   100\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   101\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   102\t\n   103\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   104\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   105\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   106\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   107\t\n   108\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   109\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   110\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   111\t\n   112\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   113\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   114\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   115\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   116\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   117\t            else:\n   118\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   119\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   120\t\n   121\t        # If stagnated for self.stagnation_boost)\n   125\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   126\t\n   127\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   128\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   129\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   130\t        else:\n   131\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   132\t\n   133\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   134\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   135\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   136\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   137\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   138\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   139\t\n   140\t        # If the self.epsilon_start))\n   145\t\n   146\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   147\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"
